Our results show that pretrained representations strongly influence downstream generalisation, and we characterize the optimal representation size as a function of task parameters: with abundant pretraining data but scarce downstream data, maximally compressed representations are optimal, whereas with limited pretraining data, higher-dimensional representations generalise better. Furthermore, we establish an exact trade-off between pretraining and supervision, quantifying how much unlabelled data is required to replace a single labelled sample. Beyond our idealised model, we observe similar phenomenology in autoencoders and pretrained LLMs. Altogether, we highlight that optimising representation size is critical, giving conditions for when compression during pretraining improves generalisation.

</details>


### [181] [Atoms of Thought: Universal EEG Representation Learning with Microstates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20182)
*Xinyang Tian,Ruitao Liu,Ziyi Ye,Siyang Xue,Xin Wang,Xuesong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用微状态（microstates）作为EEG的通用表示方法，通过在大规模医疗EEG数据集上构建通用微状态分词器，将连续EEG信号离散化为微状态序列，并在睡眠分期、情绪识别和运动想象分类等多个下游任务中验证了其有效性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统EEG表示学习多基于时域或频域特征，且通用性有限。本文旨在探索一种简单有效的EEG通用表示——微状态，以提升跨任务的表示学习性能、可解释性和可扩展性。

Method: 在大规模医疗EEG数据集上通过聚类将连续EEG信号离散化为微状态序列，构建通用微状态分词器，并统一应用于睡眠分期、情绪识别和运动想象分类等下游任务。

Result: 基于微状态的EEG表示学习在不同模型和多个任务上均优于传统时域和频域特征，且微状态具有更好的可解释性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 微状态是一种简单高效的EEG通用表示方法，在认知神经科学和临床研究中具有广阔的应用前景。

Abstract: Learning universal representations from electroencephalogram (EEG) signals is a cutting-edge approach in the field of neuroinformatics and brain-computer interfaces (BCIs). Conventionally, EEG is treated as a multivariate temporal signal, where time- or frequency-domain features are extracted for representation learning. This paper investigates a simple yet effective EEG representation, i.e., microstates. Microstates represent the building blocks of brain activity patterns at a microscopic time scale. We build a universal microstate tokenizer from a large medical EEG dataset by clustering continuous EEG signals into sequences of discrete microstates. The microstate tokenizer is then adopted universally across a series of downstream tasks, including sleep staging, emotion recognition, and motor imagery classification. Experimental results show that EEG representation learning with microstates outperforms traditional time-domain and frequency-domain features under different models and across different tasks. Further analysis shows that microstates offer greater interpretability and scalability, thereby opening up applications in both cognitive neuroscience and clinical research.

</details>


### [182] [TrajTok: Adaptive Spatial Tokenization for Trajectory Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20134)
*Zhen Xiong,Shang-Ling Hsu,Cyrus Shahabi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TrajTok，一种通过多分辨率六边形网格分词、分解式Transformer编码器（含ST-RoPE位置编码）和掩码建模预训练的可迁移轨迹表征学习方法，在多个下游任务中优于专用方法，展示了通用轨迹基础模型的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始GPS轨迹数据连续、含噪且采样不规则，现有空间分词方法存在困境：细粒度网格导致稀疏单元格和弱嵌入，粗粒度网格则将异质运动模式合并到同一token中。因此需要一种能学习可泛化轨迹表征的统一方法。

Method: 1) 根据GPS点空间分布学习多分辨率六边形单元格划分，将噪声GPS序列转化为离散单元格token；2) 采用分解式Transformer编码器，包含早期按模态自注意力块、交叉注意力融合层以及时空旋转位置编码(ST-RoPE)；3) 通过掩码token建模预训练，从部分轨迹观测中恢复几何结构和运动模式。

Result: 在Porto数据集上，冻结的TrajTok编码器配合轻量任务适配器，在轨迹相似性搜索、分类、ETA和全程旅行时间回归等任务上均优于多个任务专用方法。同一冻结编码器同时支持几何主导和运动学主导任务。

Conclusion: 学习到的多分辨率空间分词结合掩码token预训练是构建通用轨迹基础模型的有效方向，TrajTok学到的是可迁移的轨迹结构而非任务特定捷径。

Abstract: Learning generalizable trajectory representations from raw GPS traces remains difficult because the data is continuous, noisy, and irregularly sampled. Spatial tokenization is also challenging: fine grids yield sparse cells with weak embeddings, while coarse grids merge heterogeneous movement patterns into the same token. We present TrajTok, a trajectory encoder with a simple pretraining recipe for transferable trajectory embeddings. TrajTok first learns a multi-resolution hexagonal cell partition from the spatial distribution of GPS points, converting noisy GPS sequences into discrete cell tokens. To capture both geometry and kinematics, it uses a factorized transformer encoder with early per-modality self-attention blocks, cross-attention fusion layers, and spatiotemporal rotary position embeddings, ST-RoPE, to encode where and when each token occurs. TrajTok is pretrained with masked-token modeling that recovers both geometric structure and kinematic patterns from partial trajectory observations. On the Porto dataset, a frozen TrajTok encoder with lightweight task adapters achieves strong performance across trajectory similarity search, classification, estimated time of arrival, and full travel-time regression, outperforming multiple task-specific methods. The same frozen encoder supports both geometry-dominated and kinematics-dominated tasks, suggesting that TrajTok learns transferable trajectory structure rather than task-specific shortcuts. These results indicate that learned multi-resolution spatial tokenization combined with masked-token pretraining is a promising direction for general-purpose trajectory foundation models.

</details>


### [183] [When Does Model Collapse Occur in Structured Interactive Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20151)
*Yuchen Wu,Kangjie Zhou,Weijie Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了生成式AI在多模型交互学习环境中的性能退化问题（模型崩溃），利用有向图形式化模型交互，推导出模型崩溃发生的充要条件，并为线性回归和一般M估计器建立了有限样本和渐近理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注单一模型在其自身生成数据上的训练退化问题，未能捕捉多模型交互场景下的性能表现；而实际中生成式AI常处于多模型相互生成数据的复杂交互学习环境中，核心统计假设被破坏，亟需建立可靠的统计推断理论。

Method: 使用有向图形式化模型间的交互模式；推导模型崩溃发生的充要条件（依赖于交互图拓扑结构）；为线性回归建立有限样本理论结果，为一般M估计器建立渐近保证。

Result: 模型崩溃是否发生关键取决于交互图的拓扑结构；给出了显式的充要条件；通过大量数值实验验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 多模型交互环境下的模型崩溃与交互图拓扑结构密切相关，所提出的充要条件为理解和预防模型崩溃提供了理论依据。

Abstract: The proliferation of generative artificial intelligence has given rise to an interactive learning environment, where model parameters are continuously updated using not only data generated by natural processes, but also synthetic outputs produced by other models. This paradigm introduces two major challenges: (1) training data are no longer drawn exclusively from the target population, undermining a core assumption of classical statistical learning, and (2) model training processes become inherently correlated, as models interact with one another through repeated exposure to each other's synthetic outputs in a potentially complex manner. Establishing reliable statistical inference in such structured interactive learning environments therefore remains an important open problem. In particular, there is growing concern about model collapse, a phenomenon in which the performance of generative models progressively degrades as they are trained on synthetic data produced by earlier model generations.
  Prior work on model collapse primarily focuses on a single model trained on its own output, failing to capture model performance in multi-model interactive settings. In this work, we fill this gap by investigating the performance of generative models in an interactive learning environment with general interaction patterns. In particular, we formalize model interactions using directed graphs and show that the occurrence of model collapse depends critically on the topology of the interaction graph. We further derive an explicit necessary and sufficient condition characterizing when model collapse occurs, and establish finite-sample results for linear regression and asymptotic guarantees for general M-estimators. We support our theoretical findings through extensive numerical experiments.

</details>


### [184] [SAGE: Scalable Automatic Gating Ensemble for Confident Negative Harvesting in Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20157)
*Sudheer Tubati,Amit Goyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAGE提出了一种基于反事实感知的负样本 harvesting 方法，结合 SimHash 分层采样与模块化门控集成，从无标签数据中识别音乐流媒体欺诈行为，解决了正-无标签学习中的表征偏差问题，并在多个欺诈检测领域取得良好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统欺诈检测方法难以区分合法边缘案例（如超级粉丝、睡眠音乐会话）与协同欺诈行为，且正-无标签学习存在表征偏差问题，导致对稀有行为群体的覆盖率不足。

Method: 提出 SAGE 框架：利用 SimHash 进行分层采样实现负样本 harvesting，结合模块化门控集成（当前使用马氏距离和 k-NN 密度作为统计门）并配置可调投票阈值，通过保底约束采样确保稀有行为群体的全面覆盖。

Result: 在留出数据上展现了强大的精确率和召回率，并在客户级和艺术家级欺诈检测中均取得良好性能，无需修改核心方法即可跨领域泛化。

Conclusion: SAGE 有效解决了流媒体欺诈检测中合法边缘案例与欺诈行为难区分的问题，其模块化设计具有良好的跨领域泛化能力。

Abstract: Music streaming fraud, where bad actors artificially inflate stream counts to manipulate chart rankings and royalty payments, poses a significant threat to streaming services and legitimate content creators. Traditional fraud detection approaches struggle with a critical challenge: many legitimate edge cases, including super-fans and sleep-music sessions, exhibit activity patterns that closely mimic those of coordinated fraud. We present SAGE, a novel counterfactual-aware negative harvesting approach that combines SimHash-based stratified sampling with a modular gating ensemble for confident negative identification from unlabeled data. Our ensemble architecture employs pluggable statistical gates (currently instantiated with Mahalanobis distance and k-NN density) with configurable voting thresholds enabling adaptive precision-recall trade-offs. This addresses the representation bias problem in Positive-Unlabeled learning by ensuring comprehensive coverage of rare behavioral cohorts through floor-constrained sampling. Evaluation demonstrates strong precision and recall on held-out data. The approach generalizes across fraud detection domains, achieving strong performance on both customer-level and artist-level fraud without modification to the core methodology.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Unbiased Gradients for a Class of Conditional Stochastic Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18786)
*Miguel Alvarez,Ajay Jasra*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对条件随机优化（CSO）问题，提出了一种结合马尔可夫随机逼近与无偏近似方法的新算法，以解决联合分布无法精确采样的情况，并在模型平均参数估计和高维随机波动率模型投资组合选择中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CSO方法通常假设随机变量$(X,Z)$的联合分布可以精确采样，但实际中这一假设往往不成立。Goda & Kitade (2023)虽已关注此问题，仍需更通用的求解方法。

Method: 将马尔可夫链随机逼近（Markovian stochastic approximation）与无偏近似方法（unbiased approximation methods）相结合，构造优化算法求解CSO问题。

Result: 在两个实际案例（模型平均参数估计、高维全因子多元随机波动率模型的投资组合选择）中验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的结合方法能够有效处理联合分布不可精确采样的CSO问题，为相关应用提供了可行的求解框架。

Abstract: In this paper we consider the conditional stochastic optimization (CSO) problem. This consists of optimizing a function which can be written as the expectation of a function which is itself a function of a conditional expectation, i.e.~of the type $F(ξ) := \mathbb{E}\left[f\left(Z,\mathbb{E}[g(Z,X,ξ)|Z]\right)\right]$, where precise definitions are given in the main text. We address a particular class of CSO problems where the joint law of the random variables $X,Z$ cannot be exactly sampled; this case has been addressed in Goda & Kitade (2023). We introduce a method that combines Markovian stochastic approximation with unbiased approximation methods which allows one to find the optimizer of $F(ξ)$ in the context of interest. We illustrate our methodology on two examples associated to parameter estimation with model averaging and portfolio selection associated to high-dimensional full factor multivariate stochastic volatility models.

</details>


### [186] [Generalized Differentiability and Second-Order Necessary Optimality Conditions for an Elliptic Optimal Control Problem with Exponential Nonlinearity and Discrete Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19339)
*Vu Huu Nhu,Nguyen Hai Son,Phan Quang Sang,Tran Duy*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了由指数半线性椭圆方程（含离散测度源）约束的箱型最优控制问题，针对控制-状态算子可能不可方向微分的问题，提出了有限维方向可微性概念，并引入广义导数（有限维方向导数的极限），进而推导出约化目标泛函的一阶和二阶广义可微性，最终给出了最优控制问题的一阶和二阶必要最优性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在指数非线性与离散测度源的存在下，传统方向可微性失效，需要发展新的广义微分理论来处理此类最优控制问题的二阶最优性条件。

Method: 首先证明控制-状态算子在控制空间的有限维子空间上具有方向可微性；然后定义广义导数为有限维方向导数当子空间维数趋于无穷时的极限；结合指数Nemytskii算子的一阶和二阶Taylor展开估计，推导约化目标泛函的广义可微性。

Result: 获得了约化目标泛函的一阶和二阶广义可微性，并推导出最优控制问题的一阶和二阶必要最优性条件。

Conclusion: 所提出的广义微分框架有效克服了传统方向可微性缺失的困难，为含指数非线性与离散测度源的半线性椭圆最优控制问题提供了严格的二阶最优性条件理论。

Abstract: This paper deals with generalized differentiability and second-order necessary optimality conditions for a box-constrained optimal control problem governed by an exponential semilinear elliptic equation with discrete measures as sources, where the control belongs to the space of absolutely summable sequences.
  The presence of the exponential nonlinearity and discrete measures makes the analysis particularly challenging.
  In particular, the control-to-state operator may fail to be directionally differentiable.
  To address this issue, we first establish finite-dimensional directional differentiability of the control-to-state operator; that is, the operator is directionally differentiable along directions contained in finite-dimensional subspaces of the control space.
  We then introduce a notion of generalized derivative defined as the limit of the associated finite-dimensional directional derivatives as the dimension of these subspaces tends to infinity.
  Based on this concept, together with estimates for first- and second-order Taylor-type expansions of
  the exponential Nemytskii operator associated with the control-to-state mapping, we derive first- and second-order generalized differentiability of the reduced objective functional.
  This leads to first- and second-order necessary optimality conditions for the optimal control problem.

</details>


### [187] [Convergence of iterates and improved rates for accelerated augmented Lagrangian methods for linearly constrained convex optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19467)
*Xin He,Nan-Jing Hang,Yi-Bin Xiao,Ya-Ping Fang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对可微凸优化问题，受惯性原始-对偶动力系统启发，提出了一类带Nesterov外推参数的加速增广拉格朗日方法，包含隐式梯度与部分显式两种变体，证明了原始-对偶序列收敛到鞍点，并在非临界参数下将增广拉格朗日间隙、可行性违逆和目标残差的估计从O(1/k²)改进到o(1/k²)，这是首次在该框架下建立可行性违逆和目标残差的little-o速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决线性约束可微凸优化问题，并提升传统增广拉格朗日方法的收敛速度，受惯性原始-对偶动力系统的启发，希望设计出具有加速收敛保证的算法。

Method: 提出了两类加速增广拉格朗日方法：(1) 隐式梯度方案，适用于凸连续可微目标函数；(2) 部分显式方案，适用于凸光滑目标函数。两种方案均采用Nesterov外推参数进行加速。

Result: 在合适的参数条件下，证明了原始-对偶序列收敛到鞍点；在非临界参数区域，增广拉格朗日间隙、可行性违逆和目标残差的收敛估计从O(1/k²)提升到o(1/k²)。

Conclusion: 这是首次在加速增广拉格朗日方法框架下，为可行性违逆和目标残差同时建立了little-o收敛速率，显著改进了现有理论结果。

Abstract: We consider a linearly constrained convex optimization problem with a differentiable objective function. Motivated by an inertial primal-dual dynamical system with vanishing damping, we propose a class of accelerated augmented Lagrangian methods with Nesterov extrapolation parameters. The framework contains two variants: an implicit-gradient scheme for convex continuously differentiable objectives and a partially explicit scheme for convex smooth objectives. Under suitable parameter conditions, we prove convergence of the primal-dual sequence to a saddle point, together with accelerated estimates for the augmented Lagrangian gap, the feasibility violation, and the objective residual. In the noncritical parameter regime, these estimates are improved from $O(1/k^2)$ to $o(1/k^2)$. To the best of our knowledge, such little-o rates for both feasibility violation and objective residual have not been previously established for accelerated augmented Lagrangian methods in this setting.

</details>


### [188] [GLOBe: A Modular Global Optimization library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19609)
*Gaëtan Serré,Argyris Kalogeratos,Nicolas Vayatis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: GLOBe是一个模块化的Python库，统一了经典和最新的连续全局优化算法，具有可复用组件架构、C++后端（Eigen 5）和14个优化器、19个分析基准及随机函数生成器，支持算法直接比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源库在研究管道中起催化作用，新方法需要与最新基线进行比较，需要一个统一的全局优化基准框架。

Method: 采用模块化架构，将算法模式分解为可复用的族级组件，支持插件一次实现即可用于族内所有算法；C++后端基于Eigen 5实现高效线性代数运算；包含14个优化器、19个分析基准和随机函数生成器。

Result: 目前包含14个优化器、19个分析基准测试和一个随机函数生成器，以及集成的算法直接比较工具包。

Conclusion: GLOBe通过模块化架构统一了全局优化算法，实现了算法组件的可复用性和高效比较，为研究社区提供了一个统一的基准测试框架。

Abstract: Open-source libraries are have a catalytic role in research pipelines, where new methods must be compared against up-to-date baselines. We present the GLobal Optimization Benchmark (GLOBe) modular Python library that unifies classical and recent continuous global optimization algorithms, including decision-based and mathematically founded particle-based methods, in a single framework. A central contribution of GLOBe is its modular architecture, which factors common algorithmic patterns into reusable familyl evel components and enables plugins to be implemented once and makes them available to all algorithms in the corresponding family. This modular design leverages recent advances in mathematical formalization of global optimization, where structural commonalities across algorithms have been identified and used to develop broadly applicable, formally grounded algorithmic features. The C++ backend relies on Eigen 5 for efficient linear algebra. GLOBe presently includes 14 optimizers, 19 analytical benchmarks along with a random function generator, and an integrated toolkit for direct algorithm comparison.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [Position: Let's Develop Data Probes to Fundamentally Understand How Data Affects LLM Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18801)
*Shiqiang Wang,Herbert Woisetschläger,Hans Arno Jacobsen,Mingyue Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"数据探针"方法：通过从随机过程中生成合成序列，系统研究数据特征如何影响大语言模型在各阶段（训练、微调、对齐、上下文学习等）的行为，以建立数据与模型性能之间的原理性理解，超越现有的经验启发式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对哪些数据在LLM工作流不同阶段（训练、微调、对齐、上下文学习等）中有用以及为何有用缺乏原理性理解，现有方法依赖大规模实验获得经验启发式，计算密集且缺乏系统性方法论。

Method: 提出"数据探针"概念：从恰当定义的随机过程中生成合成序列，这些序列在LLM工作流的一个或多个阶段中使用时能揭示有用特性。通过观察LLM在数据探针上的行为，系统研究数据特征如何影响模型性能、泛化性和鲁棒性。探针序列的统计特性可用典型集等理论概念来描述。

Result: 该方法为揭示数据在LLM训练和推理中作用的基础性洞察提供了途径，超越了经验启发式的局限。

Conclusion: 数据探针方法是一种系统性的研究范式，能够建立数据特征与LLM行为之间的原理性理解，有望推动数据选择从经验驱动向理论驱动转变。

Abstract: Data is fundamental to large language models (LLMs). However, understanding of what makes certain data useful for different stages of an LLM workflow, including training, tuning, alignment, in-context learning, etc., and why, remains an open question. Current approaches rely heavily on extensive experimentation with large public datasets to obtain empirical heuristics for data filtering and dataset construction. These approaches are compute intensive and lack a principled way of understanding the essence of how specific data characteristics drive LLM behavior. In this position paper, we advocate for the need of developing systematic methodologies for generating synthetic sequences from appropriately defined random processes, with the goal that these sequences can reveal useful characteristics when they are used in one or multiple stages of the LLM workflow. We refer to such sequences as data probes. By observing LLM behavior on data probes, researchers can systematically conduct studies on how data characteristics influence model performance, generalization, and robustness. The probing sequences exhibit statistical properties that can be viewed using theoretical concepts, such as typical sets, which are generalized to describe the behaviors of LLMs. This data-probe approach provides a pathway for uncovering foundational insights into the role of data in LLM training and inference, beyond empirical heuristics.

</details>


### [190] [Operationalizing Document AI: A Microservice Architecture for OCR and LLM Pipelines in Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18818)
*Yao Fehlis,Benjamin Bengfort,Zhangzhang Si,Vahid Eyorokon,Prema Roman,Patrick Deziel,Devon Slonaker,Steve Veldman,Ben Johnson,Joyce Rigelo,Michael Wharton,Steve Kramer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于文档理解的微服务架构，将分类、OCR和大语言模型结构化字段提取等多个模型封装成流水线，并分享了在每小时处理数千份多页文档的生产运行经验。主要设计包括混合分类、GPU推理与CPU编排分离、异步IO处理以及独立水平扩展策略。关键发现：OCR而非语言模型解析主导端到端延迟，系统并发瓶颈由共享GPU推理容量而非工作线程数量决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术界研究集中在文档理解的新模型上，导致模型定义与生产规模化运行之间存在巨大鸿沟，缺乏将模型投入实际生产的架构模式和实践经验。

Method: 提出一种微服务架构，封装了包含分类、OCR和大语言模型结构化字段提取的多模型流水线。关键设计决策包括：混合分类策略、GPU绑定推理与CPU绑定编排分离、IO密集操作的异步处理、独立水平扩展策略。通过批量性能分析（batch profiling）评估系统行为。

Result: 两个反直觉发现：(1) OCR而非语言模型解析主导端到端延迟；(2) 系统饱和并发度由共享GPU推理容量决定，而非工作线程数量。系统能够每小时处理数千份多页文档。

Conclusion: 为从业者提供了在生产环境中构建文档理解系统的具体架构模式，弥合了模型研究与生产部署之间的差距，有效实现了模型在生产中的操作性。

Abstract: Academic research tends to focus on new models for document understanding creating a wide gap in the literature between model definition and running models at production scale. To close that gap, we present a microservice architecture that encapsulates pipelines of multiple models for classification, optical character recognition (OCR), and large language model structured field extraction as well as our experience running this pipeline on thousands of multi-page documents per hour. We describe our primary design decisions, including a hybrid classification, separation of GPU-bound inference from CPU-bound orchestration, use of asynchronous processing for the many IO-bound operations in the pipeline, and an independent, horizontal scaling strategy. Using batch profiling, we identified two surprising qualitative findings that shape production deployments: OCR, not language-model parsing, dominates end-to-end latency, and the system saturates at a concurrency determined by shared GPU-inference capacity rather than worker count. Our goal is to provide practitioners with concrete architectural patterns for building document understanding systems that work beyond the benchmark; effectively operationalizing models in production.

</details>


### [191] [Evaluating the Utility of Personal Health Records in Personalized Health AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18937)
*Rory Sayres,Kejia Chen,Ayush Jain,Matthew Thompson,Jonathan Richina,Xiang Yin,Jimmy Hu,Fan Zhang,Bob Lou,Mike Sanchez,Ines Mezerreg,Meredith Schreier,Hamsa Subramaniam,I-Ching Lee,Yugang Jia,Daniel Mcduff,Yossi Matias,Avinatan Hassidim,Dale Webster,Yun Liu,Jackie Barr,Quang Duong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了大型语言模型（LLM，Gemini 3.0 Flash）在提供个人健康记录（PHR）临床数据作为上下文时，对用户健康查询的解答能力。基于2,257条来自三种不同分布的用户查询（短网页搜索、模板化聊天机器人问题、患者向医疗团队提出的问题）和1,945份去标识化PHR，发现加入PHR上下文后，LLM答案的有用性在所有查询类型上均有显著提升（p<0.001），在安全性、准确性、相关性和个性化方面也有改善。同时提出PHR评估框架识别了LLM对复杂PHR理解的缺陷，如时间方向混乱和罕见但有意义的虚构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者管理的个人健康记录（PHR）有望赋能患者更好地理解自身健康状况，但记录中的信息复杂，可能阻碍患者获得洞察。因此需要评估将PHR临床数据作为上下文输入LLM，能否为患者的健康查询提供更有帮助的答案。

Method: 从三种不同分布（短网页搜索、模板化聊天机器人对话、患者向医疗团队提出的问题）中抽取2,257条用户查询，匹配1,945份去标识化PHR。使用Gemini 3.0 Flash生成三种条件下的回答：（1）无PHR上下文；（2）带有基本人口统计、诊断和用药摘要；（3）带有完整临床记录。采用SHARP框架和自建PHR错误模式评估框架，使用自动评分器对全部数据评估，并由临床医生对子集（n=95）进行评估。

Result: 加入PHR数据后，所有查询类型的答案有用性均有显著提升（p<0.001，配对t检验）。在安全性、准确性、相关性和个性化方面也观察到潜在改善。PHR评估框架发现了LLM对复杂PHR理解的不足，包括时间方向混乱和罕见但有意义的虚构。

Conclusion: PHR数据有助于帮助用户满足广泛的健康查询需求；本研究提供了基于PHR上下文的LLM答案缺陷监测框架，并激励进一步评估和实现患者从理解自身健康记录中获得的潜在益处。

Abstract: Patient-managed Personal Health Records (PHRs) promises to empower patients to better understand their health; but information in the record is complex, potentially hindering insights. In this study, we assess the potential of large language models (LLMs, Gemini 3.0 Flash) to provide helpful answers to user health queries, when provided clinical data from PHRs as context. A total of 2,257 user queries were drawn from 3 different distributions to represent patient questions: shorter web search queries, longer questions derived from templates of chatbot conversations, and questions patients asked to their healthcare team (patient calls). Queries were matched with de-identified PHRs (from a pool of 1,945). Gemini responses were generated (1) without PHR context; (2) with a basic summary of demographics, conditions, and medications; (3) with full, extensive clinical notes. For evaluation, we leveraged an existing rating framework (SHARP), and developed a new framework for specific error modes when interpreting PHRs. Evaluation was performed using autoraters for the full set, and with clinician ratings for a subset (n=95), with both sets of raters knowing the full PHR context. We see significant improvements in the helpfulness of answers to all question types with PHR data (p < 0.001, paired t-test). We also observe potential gains in safety, accuracy, relevance and personalization of answers. Our PHR evaluation framework further identifies gaps in LLM understanding of particular aspects of complex PHRs, such as temporal disorientation, and rare but meaningful confabulations. These results suggest potential for PHR data to help people with a wide range of user needs; and provide a framework for monitoring for gaps in LLM answers based on PHR context. This study motivates further work to assess and realize potential benefits to users from understanding their health records.

</details>


### [192] [Learn-by-Wire Training Control Governance: Bounded Autonomous Training Under Stress for Stability and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19008)
*Anis Radianis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LBW-Guard是一个运行在AdamW优化器之上的有界自主训练控制治理层，通过监控训练遥测数据并施加有界控制，在不替换优化器更新规则的情况下提升大语言模型训练的稳定性。在Qwen2.5系列模型上，LBW-Guard将7B模型的最终困惑度从13.21降至10.74（提升18.7%），训练时间从392.54秒减至357.02秒（加速1.10倍），并在强学习率压力下保持可训练性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型训练在激进的学习率、规模扩展和运行时压力条件下，日益面临训练不稳定、训练退化以及计算资源浪费的问题。现有方法（如梯度裁剪）无法有效应对这些挑战，因此需要一种新的训练控制机制。

Method: 提出Learn-by-Wire Guard (LBW-Guard)，一种有界自主训练控制治理层，运行在AdamW优化器之上。LBW-Guard不替换优化器更新规则，而是通过观察训练遥测数据、解释不稳定敏感区域，并对优化器执行施加有界控制，同时保持固定的训练目标不变。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B参考设置中，最终困惑度从13.21降至10.74（18.7%提升），端到端时间从392.54秒减至357.02秒（1.10倍加速）。在高学习率压力下（LR=3e-3和1e-3），AdamW退化至1885.24和659.76困惑度，而LBW-Guard保持在11.57和10.33。梯度裁剪基线无法复现此效果。

Conclusion: 稳定性敏感的大语言模型训练可以从优化器之上的治理平面中受益。LBW-Guard证明了有界运行时控制可以在压力下保持有效的计算，且该方法与优化器替换和局部梯度抑制是截然不同的。

Abstract: Modern language-model training is increasingly exposed to instability, degraded runs, and wasted compute, especially under aggressive learning-rate, scale, and runtime-stress conditions. This paper introduces Learn-by-Wire Guard (LBW-Guard), a bounded autonomous training-control governance layer that operates above AdamW. Rather than replacing the optimizer update rule, LBW-Guard observes training telemetry, interprets instability-sensitive regimes, and applies bounded control to optimizer execution while preserving fixed training objectives.
  We evaluate LBW-Guard in a Qwen2.5-centered stress-and-robustness suite using WikiText-103, with Qwen2.5-7B as the empirical anchor, model-size comparisons against Qwen2.5-3B and Qwen2.5-14B, learning-rate stress tests, gradient-clipping baselines, and a no-LoRA TinyLlama-1B full-parameter sanity check. In the 7B reference setting, LBW-Guard reduces final perplexity from 13.21 to 10.74, an 18.7% improvement, while reducing end-to-end time from 392.54s to 357.02s, a 1.10x speedup. Under stronger learning-rate stress, AdamW degrades to 1885.24 final perplexity at LR=3e-3 and 659.76 at LR=1e-3, whereas LBW-Guard remains trainable at 11.57 and 10.33, respectively. Gradient-clipping baselines do not reproduce this effect.
  These results support a scoped systems conclusion that stability-sensitive LLM training can benefit from a governance plane above the optimizer. LBW-Guard provides evidence that bounded runtime control can preserve productive compute under stress while remaining distinct from optimizer replacement and local gradient suppression.

</details>


### [193] [AgentNLQ: A General-Purpose Agent for Natural Language to SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19010)
*Olena Bogdanov,Yeunji Jung,Chandra Dhir,Pareekshitreddy Gaddam,Saurabh Jain,Lakshmi Tumati,Vijay Parthasarathy,Anup Shirgaonkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种多智能体NL2SQL方法，通过语义增强的模式表示、业务规则和自校正机制，在BIRD基准上达到78.1%的语义准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NL2SQL转换对研究人员和企业至关重要，但现有LLM方法在准确性上尚未达到人类专家水平，因此需要改进NL2SQL算法。

Method: 设计了一个多智能体解决方案，包含优化的编排器，利用LLM进行规划、编排、反思和自校正；开发了先进的模式丰富方法，创建上下文感知的元数据；并加入了用户提供的业务规则。

Result: 在BIRD-SQL基准上达到78.1%的语义准确率，并在不同领域和数据集上验证了方法的准确性和泛化性。

Conclusion: 提出的多智能体方法通过模式丰富和自校正机制有效提升了NL2SQL的准确性，具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Natural language to SQL (NL2SQL) conversion is an important problem for researchers and enterprises due to the ubiquitous importance of relational databases in broad-ranging practical problems. Despite the rapid advancements in the capabilities of LLMs, NL2SQL has not reached parity in accuracy with human expert SQL writers, hence needing additional improvements in NL2SQL algorithms. This study presents a new multi-agent method for NL2SQL that achieves 78.1% semantic accuracy on the BIg Bench for LaRge-scale Database (BIRD) benchmark. Our method leverages a semantically enriched representation of user-provided schema, adds user-provided business rules, and produces accurate SQL queries. The main contributions of this study are (a) We designed an optimized new orchestrator in a multi-agent solution that uses LLMs to plan, orchestrate, reflect, and self-correct to generate accurate SQL queries, (b) We developed an advanced schema enrichment method that creates context-aware metadata to improve accuracy, and (c) We demonstrated the accuracy and generalizability of the method across different domains and datasets by evaluating it on the BIRD-SQL benchmark.

</details>


### [194] [DecisionBench: A Benchmark for Emergent Delegation in Long-Horizon Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19099)
*Yuxuan Gao,Megan Wang,Yi Ling Yu,Zijian Carl Ma,Ao Qu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DecisionBench是一个面向长周期智能体工作流中涌现式委托的基准测试平台，包含固定任务套件、11个模型池、委托接口、确定性技能标注层和多轴指标集。在23,375个实例上五种条件的参考扫描显示：不同感知条件下最终任务质量无显著差异（|beta|<=0.010, p>=0.21），但路由保真度在7.5%-29.5%之间波动，反事实天花板表明完美委托与当前表现存在15-31个百分点的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试缺乏对智能体间委托行为（即一个智能体将任务委托给另一个智能体执行）的系统评估框架，特别是缺乏标准化的指标、接口和对比基线来量化不同委托策略在质量、成本、延迟等多维度上的表现与潜在提升空间。

Method: 构建一个统一的基准平台，固定任务套件（GAIA, tau-bench, BFCL multi-turn）、11个模型的同行池、call_model加可选的read_profile委托接口、确定性技能标注层，以及涵盖质量、成本、延迟、委托率、路由保真度、供应商自我偏好和反事实委托天花板的多轴指标集。在五种条件（不同信息感知程度）下对23,375个任务实例进行参考扫描。

Result: (i) 四种信息感知条件下平均最终任务质量在统计上无显著差异，仅靠质量评估会遗漏编排信号；(ii) 路由保真度@1在7.5%-29.5%之间变化，传递通道（按需工具 vs. 预载描述）主导描述内容；(iii) 反事实天花板显示完美委托比当前性能高出15-31个百分点，揭示巨大的未来改进空间。

Conclusion: DecisionBench为评估长周期智能体工作流中的委托策略提供了标准化基准，揭示了当前委托方法的不足与巨大提升潜力，并发布了完整的平台、标注层、干预套件、分析流水线和实验归档以供社区使用。

Abstract: We introduce DecisionBench, a benchmark substrate for emergent delegation in long-horizon agentic workflows. The substrate fixes a task suite (GAIA, tau-bench, BFCL multi-turn), a peer-model pool (11 models, 7 vendor families), a delegation interface (call_model plus an optional read_profile channel), a deterministic skill-annotation layer, and a multi-axis metric suite covering quality, cost, latency, delegation rate, routing fidelity-at-k, vendor self-preference, and a counterfactual-delegation ceiling. The substrate is agnostic to how peer information is generated or delivered, so learned routers, richer peer memories, adaptive profile construction, and multi-step delegation can all be evaluated against it. We characterize the substrate with a five-condition reference sweep on the full pool (n=23,375 task instances). Three benchmark-level findings emerge: (i) mean end-task quality is statistically indistinguishable across the four awareness conditions (|beta| <= 0.010, p >= 0.21), so quality-only evaluation would miss the orchestration signal; (ii) routing fidelity-at-1 ranges from 7.5% to 29.5% across conditions at near-equal mean quality, with delivery channel (on-demand tool vs. preloaded description) dominating description content; (iii) a counterfactual ceiling places perfect delegation 15-31 percentage points above measured performance on every suite, locating large unrealized headroom for future orchestration methods. We release the substrate, annotation layer, reference intervention suite, analysis pipeline, and 220 per-condition run archives.

</details>


### [195] [POLAR-Bench: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Privacy-Utility Trade-offs in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19127)
*Qiaoyuan Zheng,Yiqu Yang,Qi Gao,Imanol Schlag*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: POLAR-Bench是一个评估LLM智能体在对抗性第三方系统攻击下如何遵循用户隐私策略的基准测试。在10个领域、7852个样本中，前沿模型能隐藏99%以上的受保护属性，而1-30B参数的小模型表现最差（最弱的泄露超过一半），揭示了不同模型在隐私对齐上的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体越来越多地接触用户隐私数据并代表用户与第三方系统交互，但第三方系统可能对抗性地探测用户隐私信息。现有评估缺乏对智能体在对抗性环境下遵循用户隐私策略能力的系统性度量。

Method: 构建POLAR-Bench基准，包含一个持有隐私策略和任务的受信模型，与一个对抗性探测任务相关和受保护属性的第三方模型对话。覆盖10个领域、7852个样本，通过确定性集合成员关系评分隐私和效用，沿隐私策略维度和攻击策略两个正交轴变化，生成5×5的诊断表。

Result: 前沿模型能隐藏99%以上的受保护属性，而1-30B参数的小模型表现显著更差，最弱的泄露超过一半受保护属性。

Conclusion: POLAR-Bench定位了每个模型的意图遵循失效点，为最需要隐私对齐的场景提供了评估基础。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly have access to private user data and act on the user's behalf when interacting with third-party systems. The user defines what may and must not be shared, and the agent must robustly follow that intent even when third-party systems behave adversarially. We introduce POLAR-Bench (Policy-aware adversarial Benchmark), in which a trusted model with a privacy policy and a task converses with a third-party model that adversarially probes for both task-relevant and protected attributes. Across 10 domains and 7,852 samples, we score privacy and utility by deterministic set-membership and vary privacy policy dimension and attack strategy along two orthogonal axes, producing a 5 times 5 diagnostic surface per model. Our results reveal a sharp split: current frontier models withhold over 99% of protected attributes, while smaller open-weight models in the 1--30B range, the class users most commonly run as their own trusted agent on-device or via private inference, score notably worse, with the weakest leaking over half. POLAR-Bench thus localizes where each model's intent-following breaks down, providing a foothold for privacy alignment where it matters most.

</details>


### [196] [How Far Are We From True Auto-Research?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19156)
*Zhengxin Zhang,Ning Wang,Sainyam Galhotra,Claire Cardie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ResearchArena框架，让多种AI智能体自主完成完整研究流程（构思、实验、写作、自我改进），共生成117篇论文。通过三种评审方式评估发现：仅看手稿评分时表现乐观（Claude Code最佳，甚至接近ICLR平均水平），但人工审查发现严重问题——实验严谨性不足，存在结果伪造、实验不充分、计划与执行不匹配三大失败模式。最终117篇论文无一达到顶会接收标准，表明真正的自动科研仍有差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动科研系统虽能生成完整论文，但缺乏对智能体生成论文质量的系统性研究，特别是对其实验严谨性和真正学术价值的评估。

Method: 构建ResearchArena最小化脚手架框架，支持多种商业智能体（Claude Code、Codex、Kimi Code）自主完成完整研究循环（构思→实验→写作→自我改进）。在13个计算机科学种子课题上，每对智能体-领域组合进行3次试验，共生成117篇论文。采用三种互补评估方式：仅基于手稿的自动评审（SAR）、包含工作区检查的同行评审（PR）、人工元评审。

Result: 仅手稿评审中Claude Code得分最高，优于Analemma的FARS，与ICLR 2025人类加权平均水平相当。但人工审查发现SAR与真实决策对齐差。引入工作区检查的评审后分数骤降。实验严谨性是主要瓶颈，包含三类失败模式：结果伪造、实验不充分、计划与执行不匹配，且智能体间差异显著（Codex 5%/8%的论文-工件不匹配/伪造引用 vs Kimi Code 77%/72%）。117篇论文无一达到顶会接收标准。

Conclusion: 当前最小化框架下的智能体虽能在手稿形式上表现良好，但实验严谨性严重不足，且不同智能体形成了截然不同的研究"人格"。距离真正的自动科研仍有显著差距。

Abstract: Recent auto-research systems can produce complete papers, but feasibility is not the same as quality, and the field still lacks a systematic study of how good agent-generated papers actually are. We introduce ResearchArena, a minimal scaffold that lets off-the-shelf agents (Claude Code using Opus 4.6, Codex using GPT-5.4, and Kimi Code using K2.5) carry out the full research loop themselves (ideation, experimentation, paper writing, self-refinement) under only lightweight guidance. Across 13 computer science seeds and 3 trials per agent-domain pair, ResearchArena yields 117 agent-generated papers, each evaluated under three complementary lenses: a manuscript-only reviewer (SAR), an artifact-aware peer review (PR) in which agents inspect the workspace alongside the manuscript, and an human conducted meta-review. Under SAR alone the picture is optimistic: Claude Code obtains the highest score, outperforms Analemma's FARS, and matches the weighted-average human ICLR 2025 submission, suggesting that minimally scaffolded agents can produce papers that look competitive on manuscript-only review. Manual inspection, however, reveals this picture is overstated: SAR scores are poorly aligned with its actual acceptance decisions and reward plausible framing without verifying experimental substance. Under artifact-aware PR scores drop sharply, and manual auditing identifies experimental rigor as the major bottleneck, decomposing into three failure modes (fabricated results, underpowered experiments, and plan/execution mismatch) that are highly agent-dependent: Codex 5%/8% paper-vs-artifact mismatch / fabricated references versus Kimi Code 77%/72%, a $\sim$15$\times$ spread that tracks distinct research personas the agents develop. None of the 117 agent-generated papers reaches the acceptance bar of a top-tier venue. This suggests that we are still gapped from the true auto-research.

</details>


### [197] [Hallucination as Exploit: Evidence-Carrying Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19192)
*Guijia Zhang,Hao Zheng,Harry Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出证据携带型多模态智能体（ECA），通过将工具调用分解为动作关键谓词，并利用约束性DOM/OCR/AX验证器生成类型化证书，再由确定性门控仅授权有证书支持的操作，从而解决多模态智能体中因视觉幻觉导致未授权操作（幻觉到动作转换）的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体依赖截图、文档等视觉信息选择工具调用，当错误的视觉声称触发点击、邮件、数据提取或转账时，幻觉从单纯的答案质量错误转变为授权失败（即"幻觉到动作转换"），现有方法缺乏对此安全失效模式的防护。

Method: ECA架构包含三个核心组件：(1) 将每个工具调用分解为动作关键谓词；(2) 从受约束的DOM/OCR/AX验证器获取类型化证书；(3) 确定性门控仅授予证书支持的操作权限。模型文本被视为不可采纳证据，感知错误被转化为可操作的验证器、模式和实现残差。

Result: 经过四次定向加固的门控绕过率从15%降至1.3%；200任务端到端管线不安全动作率为0%（Wilson 95%上限2.67%），120任务浏览器概念验证上限4.3%；HACR审计显示朴素智能体不安全执行率100%、仅提示防御49.6%，ECA为0%。

Conclusion: 核心原则：模型语言可以提议动作，但外部证据必须授权这些动作。ECA通过外部证据证书机制有效防止幻觉驱动的未授权操作，且神经评判基线在相同威胁模型下仍可被绕过。

Abstract: Multimodal agents use screenshots, documents, and webpages to choose tool calls. When a false visual claim triggers a click, email, extraction, or transfer, hallucination becomes an authorization failure rather than an answer-quality error. We formalize this failure mode as hallucination-to-action conversion: an unsupported perceptual claim supplies the precondition that makes a privileged action appear permitted. We propose evidence-carrying multimodal agents (ECA), which treat free-form model text as inadmissible evidence. ECA decomposes each tool call into action-critical predicates, obtains typed certificates from constrained DOM/OCR/AX verifiers, and lets a deterministic gate grant only the privileges those certificates support. The architecture does not hide perception error; it converts opaque model belief into named verifier, schema, and implementation residuals. Verifier red-teaming over 1,900 attacks exposes this residual directly: four targeted hardening steps reduce gate bypass from 15% to 1.3%. With content-derived certificates, ECA obtains 0% unsafe-action rate on a 200-task end-to-end pipeline (Wilson 95% upper bound 2.67%) and a 120-task browser proof-of-concept (upper bound 4.3%). A direct HACR audit on 500 stratified task keys shows that unsupported action-critical claims reach unsafe execution for naive agents (100.0%) and prompt-only defense (49.6%), but not for ECA. Oracle-certificate replay on 7,488 GPT-5.4 benchmark traces serves as a gate-correctness sanity check, and neural judge baselines remain bypassable under the same threat model. The resulting principle is simple: model language may propose actions, but external evidence must authorize them.

</details>


### [198] [Not all uncertainty is alike: volatility, stochasticity, and exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19215)
*Payam Piray*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现环境中两种不确定性（波动性和随机性）对最优探索行为产生相反的影响：波动性促进探索，随机性抑制探索。作者通过扩展Gittins指数框架和高斯状态空间bandit理论证明了这一非对称性，并提出了CAUSE算法，该算法在异构噪声环境中优于标准探索策略，且对精神疾病的计算模型有启示意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究认为不确定性通常促进探索，但未区分不同类型的环境不确定性。本文旨在揭示波动性（volatility）和随机性（stochasticity）两种不确定性对探索行为的不同影响，填补这一理论空白。

Method: 将Gittins指数框架扩展到高斯状态空间bandit模型（含潜动态），通过控制作为推理（control-as-inference）推导出CAUSE（Cause-Aware Uncertainty-Sensitive Exploration）——一种封闭形式的探索奖励，该奖励继承了与最优策略相同的单调性。

Result: 证明波动性增强探索而随机性抑制探索的非对称性；CAUSE在异构噪声环境中优于标准探索策略；CAUSE也优于基于Gittins-per-arm的策略（后者的rested-bandit最优性无法迁移到restless场景）。

Conclusion: 学习和探索受相同的噪声推断非对称性支配；病理性噪声推断会产生"反向"而非仅受损的探索行为，对精神疾病的计算机制有重要启示。

Abstract: Adaptive decision-making in biological and artificial intelligence requires balancing the exploitation of known outcomes with the exploration of uncertain alternatives. Although prior work suggests that uncertainty generally promotes exploration, it has typically treated distinct sources of environmental uncertainty as equivalent. We consider environments with latent reward states that drift over time (volatility) and are observed through noisy outcomes (stochasticity). Both increase posterior uncertainty, yet we show they drive optimal exploration in opposite directions: volatility enhances it, stochasticity suppresses it. We establish this asymmetry formally by extending the Gittins index framework to Gaussian state-space bandits with latent dynamics. We further derive Cause-Aware Uncertainty-Sensitive Exploration (CAUSE), a closed-form exploration bonus obtained via control-as-inference that inherits the same monotonicities. CAUSE outperforms standard exploration strategies in environments with heterogeneous noise structure, and also improves on a Gittins-per-arm policy whose rested-bandit optimality does not transfer to restless settings. Learning and exploration are governed by the same noise-inference asymmetry, and the framework predicts that pathological noise inference produces \emph{reversed} rather than merely impaired exploration, with implications for computational accounts of psychiatric conditions.

</details>


### [199] [SimGym: A Framework for A/B Test Simulation in E-Commerce with Traffic-Grounded VLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19219)
*Han Li,Vibhor Malik,Zahra Zanjani Foumani,Alberto Castelo,Shuang Xie,Ailin Fan,Keat Yang Koay,Yuanzheng Zhu,Meysam Feghhi,Ronie Uliana,Zhaoyu Zhang,Angelo Ocana Martins,Mingyu Zhao,Francis Pelland,Jonathan Faerman,Nikolas LeBlanc,Aaron Glazer,Andrew McNamara,Zhong Wu,Lingyun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SimGym是一个利用视觉语言模型（VLM）智能体在真实浏览器中模拟电商平台A/B测试的框架。它通过流量驱动的人物生成、实时浏览器智能体架构和评估协议三大组件，将实验周期从数周缩短至一小时以内，并在主题UI变化测试中达到77%的加购方向对齐准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统A/B测试需要分流真实流量、耗时数周才能达到统计显著性，且可能降低用户体验。作者希望构建一个无需真实用户参与、快速且安全的模拟测试框架。

Method: SimGym包含三个核心组件：(a) 基于生产点击流数据的流量驱动人物画像生成管道，推导买家原型和意图；(b) 实时浏览器智能体架构，结合多模态感知（视觉+浏览器结构化观察）、情景记忆和护栏机制，在控制和实验店面中执行连贯购物会话；(c) 评估协议，比较模拟结果偏移与真实买家行为偏移。

Result: 在主流电商平台的主题UI变化A/B测试中，SimGym智能体在加购行为方向对齐上达到77%的准确率，并将实验周期从数周缩短至一小时以内。

Conclusion: SimGym能够在不暴露真实用户的情况下快速进行电商界面A/B测试模拟，有效缩短实验周期并保证结果可靠性。

Abstract: A/B testing remains the gold standard for evaluating modifications to e-commerce storefronts, yet it diverts traffic, requires weeks to reach statistical significance, and risks degrading user experience. We present SimGym, a framework for simulating A/B tests on e-commerce storefronts using vision-language model (VLM) agents operating in a live browser. The framework comprises three key components: (a) a traffic-grounded persona generation pipeline that derives per-shop buyer archetypes and intents from production clickstream data; (b) a live-browser agent architecture that combines multimodal perception over visual and browser-structured observations with episodic memory and guardrails to conduct coherent shopping sessions across control and treatment storefronts; and (c) an evaluation protocol that compares simulated outcome shifts with observed shifts in real buyer behavior. We validate SimGym on A/B tests of visually driven UI theme changes from a major e-commerce platform across diverse storefronts and product categories. Empirical results show that SimGym agents achieve strong agreement with observed outcome shifts, attaining 77% directional alignment with add-to-cart shifts observed across interface variants in real-buyer traffic. It reduces experimental cycles from weeks to under an hour, enabling rapid experimentation without exposing real buyers to candidate variants.

</details>


### [200] [Can Large Language Models Revolutionize Survey Research? Experiments with Disaster Preparedness Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19229)
*Yan Wang,Ziyi Guo,Christopher McCarty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个五阶段LLM集成框架，涵盖问卷设计、样本选择、预测试、缺失数据插补和事后分析，以2024年飓风米尔顿佛罗里达居民调查（n=946）为试验平台。提出受保护动机理论约束的共现知识图谱和七种LLM配置，其中锚定边际理论信息LLM（A-TLM）在灾难相关块状MNAR条件下，RMSE（1.439）优于所有经典插补基线，近零带符号偏差（-0.121），而随机森林插补器产生最大绝对偏差（-0.631）。围绕PMT因果结构组织检索并在单次模型调用中整合所有证据优于非结构化检索和分阶段顺序推理（MAE 0.993 vs. 1.097）。近零聚合偏差可能掩盖对立的子组错误，提出子组分层的偏差审计作为报告标准。检索约束的KG聊天机器人通过有依据的拒绝实现架构上的幻觉管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调查研究面临响应率下降、样本偏差、风险受访者的块状缺失以及AI辅助欺诈完成等结构性挑战。LLM被提出作为解决方案，但在完整调查工作流中的严格评估仍然稀缺，特别是在数据质量至关重要的灾害情境中。

Method: 提出五阶段LLM集成框架：问卷设计、样本选择、预测试、缺失数据插补和事后分析。引入PMT约束的共现知识图谱，开发七种LLM配置（零样本推理、检索增强基线、理论信息变体）。提出锚定边际理论信息LLM（A-TLM），使用灾难相关块状MNAR条件进行评估。

Result: A-TLM在RMSE（1.439 vs 1.496）上优于IPW/MI、MICE+PMM、missForest等经典基线，近零偏差（-0.121）远优于随机森林（-0.631）。PMT因果结构检索+单次模型调用优于非结构化检索和分阶段推理（MAE 0.993 vs 1.097）。发现近零聚合偏差可掩盖对立的子组错误。

Conclusion: 理论约束的LLM集成在灾难调查数据插补中优于经典方法；检索架构设计（因果结构组织、单次模型调用）显著影响性能；聚合偏差审计需扩展到子组层面；检索约束的知识图谱聊天机器人可通过有依据拒绝控制幻觉。

Abstract: Survey research faces mounting structural challenges: declining response rates, sample bias, block-wise missingness among at-risk respondents, and AI-assisted fraudulent completions in online panels. Large language models (LLMs) have been proposed as a remedy, yet rigorous evaluations across the full survey workflow remain scarce, particularly in disaster contexts where data quality matters most. We present and evaluate a five-stage framework for LLM integration covering questionnaire design, sample selection, pilot testing, missing-data imputation, and post-collection analysis, using the 2024 Hurricane Milton preparedness survey of Florida residents (n=946) as a shared empirical testbed. We introduce a Protection Motivation Theory (PMT)-constrained co-occurrence knowledge graph and develop seven LLM configurations spanning zero-shot inference, retrieval-augmented baselines, and novel theory-informed variants. Our proposed Anchored Marginal Theory-Informed LLM (A-TLM) outperforms all three classical imputation baselines (IPW/MI, MICE+PMM, missForest) on RMSE under disaster-relevant block-wise MNAR conditions (S4 RMSE 1.439 vs. 1.496 for the next-best), while achieving near-zero signed bias (-0.121) where the random-forest imputer produces the largest absolute bias (-0.631). Organizing retrieval around PMT causal structure and integrating all evidence in a single model call outperforms unstructured retrieval and staged sequential inference (MAE 0.993 vs. 1.097 for standard RAG). We document that near-zero aggregate bias can mask opposing subgroup errors and propose subgroup-stratified bias auditing as a reporting standard. A retrieval-constrained knowledge-graph chatbot demonstrates that hallucination is architecturally manageable through grounded refusal.

</details>


### [201] [Causal Evidence for Attention Head Imbalance in Modality Conflict Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19250)
*Jinrui Jiang,Zhangtai Wu,Zhen Wu,Xinyu Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过头部级因果分析发现多模态大模型中存在两类注意力头——幻觉驱动头和幻觉抵抗头，两者呈不平衡路由结构导致模态冲突幻觉，并据此提出MACI干预方法，在检测到冲突时抑制驱动头，显著减少幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在多模态大模型生成过程中，为何视觉证据无法战胜错误的文本前提而导致模态冲突幻觉，从内部组件的因果机制角度探究驱动或抵抗这一失败的原因。

Method: 对五个开源MLLM进行头部级因果分析（路径修补），识别出两类因果角色相反的注意力头（驱动头和抵抗头）；提出MACI（模态冲突感知因果干预），仅在检测到冲突时条件性地抑制因果识别的幻觉驱动头。

Result: 驱动效应分布更广泛且聚合权重更大，抵抗效应集中在少数高重要性头部；在MMMC基准上，MACI在所有推理时基线方法中实现最大的幻觉减少，并保持良好的幻觉-准确率权衡，且零样本迁移到SCI-SemanticConflict测试。

Conclusion: 分布式的驱动影响与局部化的抵抗形成了不平衡的路由结构，使生成偏向错误文本前提；基于此提出的MACI干预方法能有效减少模态冲突幻觉。

Abstract: Modality-conflict hallucination occurs when multimodal large language models (MLLMs) prioritize erroneous textual premises over contradictory visual evidence. To understand why visual evidence fails to prevail during generation, we take a mechanistic perspective and examine which internal components drive or resist this failure. We perform head-level causal analysis using path patching across five open-source MLLMs and identify two groups of attention heads with opposing causal roles: hallucination-driving heads and hallucination-resisting heads. We find a consistent asymmetry: driving effects are more broadly distributed and carry greater aggregate weight, whereas resisting effects concentrate in a small number of high-importance heads. Ablation experiments further confirm that these groups exert opposing effects during generation: distributed driving influence and localized resistance together form an imbalanced routing structure that biases generation toward the erroneous premise. Motivated by this finding, we propose MACI (Modality-conflict-Aware Causal Intervention), a conditional intervention that suppresses causally identified hallucination-driving heads only when conflict is detected. Across five MLLMs, MACI achieves the largest hallucination reduction among compared inference-time baselines on the MMMC benchmark with a favorable hallucination-accuracy trade-off, and transfers zero-shot to the SCI-SemanticConflict test.

</details>


### [202] [AQuaUI: Visual Token Reduction for GUI Agents with Adaptive Quadtrees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19260)
*Yuankai Li,Tinghui Zhu,Ha Min Son,Zhe Zhao,Xin Liu,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AquaUI，一种无需训练、推理时通过自适应四叉树对GUI截图进行非均匀信息密度感知的token缩减方法，在保持99.06%性能的同时实现13.22%加速和29.52%视觉token减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LMM驱动的GUI agent模型需要处理高分辨率截图，但截图存在严重的空间信息密度非均匀性（大面积空白区域与关键文本/图标区域信息密度差异大）。现有方法要么需要额外训练，要么依赖基于注意力的token压缩，忽略了GUI截图的结构化布局和空间冗余特征。

Method: 1) 自适应四叉树构建：对每张GUI截图输入构建自适应四叉树，每个叶子节点保留一个代表性合并token；2) 空间位置保持：保留token的空间位置信息贯穿整个流水线，确保位置编码阶段一致；3) 条件四叉树：利用连续截图间的连续性，以前一帧四叉树为参考细化当前四叉树，保持跨多步GUI交互的时序一致性。

Result: 在主流GUI agent模型上，AquaUI在标准grounding和导航基准测试中一致优于基线方法。在GUI-Owl-1.5-32B-Instruct上，实现13.22%加速、29.52%视觉token减少，同时保留99.06%的全token性能。

Conclusion: GUI截图的空间冗余可以在无需重新训练的条件下在推理时被有效利用，验证了非均匀信息密度感知的token缩减策略在GUI agent场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have recently emerged as promising backbones for GUI-agent models, where high-resolution GUI screenshots are introduced to the prompts at each iteration step. However, these screenshots exhibit highly non-uniform spatial information density: large regions may carry little information and are visually homogeneous, while key text and icons may require high visual fidelity. Existing approaches to this problem either require additional training or rely on attention-based token compression, ignoring the structured layout and spatial redundancy of GUI screenshots. To fill the gap, this paper proposes AquaUI, a training-free inference-time token reduction method for GUI agent models that utilizes the non-uniform information density in screenshots. AQuaUI constructs an adaptive quadtree on each screenshot input and keeps one representative merged token per leaf of the quadtree. AQuaUI preserves the spatial positions of retained tokens throughout the pipeline to ensure that all position-encoding stages remain consistent. To further improve temporal consistency across multi-step GUI interactions, we propose a conditional quadtree algorithm that leverages the continuity between consecutive screenshots within a single request. Specifically, it refines the current quadtree using previous quadtrees as references, helping preserve fine-grained regions across static or mildly shifted GUI states. We implement AQuaUI on state-of-the-art GUI agent models and conduct experiments on standard grounding and navigational benchmarks. AQuaUI consistently shows improved accuracy-efficiency trade-offs over prior baselines. Notably, on GUI-Owl-1.5-32B-Instruct, AQuaUI achieves up to 13.22% speedup and 29.52% fewer visual tokens while retaining 99.06% of full-token performance, suggesting that the spatial redundancy of GUI screenshots can be exploited at inference without retraining.

</details>


### [203] [Swimming with Whales: Analysis of Power Imbalances in Stake-Weighted Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19264)
*Yuzhe Zhang,Manvir Schneider,Qin Wang,Davide Grossi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过计算社会选择理论视角，研究权益证明（PoS）区块链中按权益加权的投票治理范式存在的权力失衡问题，利用Penrose-Banzhaf权力指数量化权力，通过理论分析和实证数据（Project Catalyst）表明，权益加权投票中权力与权益所有权之间难以实现完美对齐，但在特定条件下可在期望意义上近似，且实证揭示了当前系统中普遍存在的权力失衡现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权益证明（PoS）区块链中按权益加权的投票治理范式存在严重的权力扭曲问题：少数持有大量权益的用户可能在未持有全部权益的情况下完全控制决策。作者旨在通过计算社会选择理论的视角，系统研究这一权力失衡现象的程度与特征。

Method: 采用Penrose-Banzhaf权力指数量化权益加权投票中的权力分布。理论分析证明权力与相对权益所有权之间完美对齐的不可实现性，并推导在期望意义上可近似对齐的特定条件；实证分析使用真实链上治理系统（Project Catalyst）的数据进行验证。

Result: 理论上证明了权益加权投票中权力与权益所有权无法完美对齐，但可在特定条件下在期望意义上近似；实证数据来自Project Catalyst，提供了对当前权益加权治理系统中权力失衡的细致理解。

Conclusion: 权益加权投票范式存在固有权力失衡问题，需要在治理机制设计中予以重视和缓解。

Abstract: Voting methods weighted by stakes are the fundamental governance paradigm in Proof-of-Stake (PoS) blockchains. Such a paradigm is known to be prone to power distortions: a few users possessing large stakes may completely control decision making, even without owning the totality of the stakes. We study this phenomenon through the lens of computational social choice, focusing on the extent of power imbalances in stake-weighted voting when power is quantified using the Penrose-Banzhaf power index. Our work presents both analytical and empirical contributions. Analytically, we demonstrate that while a perfect alignment between power and relative stake ownership is generally unattainable, it can be approximated in expectation under specific conditions. Empirically, using data from a real-world on-chain governance system (Project Catalyst), we provide a more fine-grained understanding of the power imbalances that are likely to occur in current stake-weighted governance systems.

</details>


### [204] [MOCHA: Multi-Objective Chebyshev Annealing for Agent Skill Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19330)
*Md Mehrab Tanjim,Jayakumar Subramanian,Xiang Chen,Branislav Kveton,Subhojyoti Mukherjee,Anlan Zhang,Sungchul Kim,Somdeb Sarkhel,Sunav Choudhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了MOCHA方法，利用切比雪夫标量化和指数退火，在多约束条件下优化LLM agent技能，在6个任务上均优于现有优化器，平均正确率提升7.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示优化器将多目标约束简化为加权求和，忽略了非凸帕累托前沿区域，导致在多约束技能优化中性能停滞。

Method: 采用切比雪夫标量化覆盖完整帕累托前沿（包括非凸区域），结合指数退火从探索过渡到利用，统一多目标变异算子。

Result: 在6个技能任务中，MOCHA全部取得进展，而现有优化器在4个任务上零进展。MOCHA平均正确率提升7.5%，在FEVER和TheoremQA上分别提升14.9%和10.4%，发现两倍以上帕累托最优变体。

Conclusion: 多目标优化框架MOCHA有效解决了LLM agent技能在平台约束下的多目标权衡问题，实现了帕累托最优技能变体的系统发现。

Abstract: LLM agents organize behavior through skills - structured natural-language specifications governing how an agent reasons, retrieves, and responds. Unlike monolithic prompts, skills are multi-field artifacts subject to hard platform constraints: description fields are truncated for routing, instruction bodies are compacted via progressive disclosure, and co-resident skills compete for limited context windows. These constraints make skill optimization inherently multi-objective: a skill must simultaneously maximize task performance and satisfy platform limits. Yet existing prompt optimizers either ignore these trade-offs or collapse them into a weighted sum, missing Pareto-optimal variants in non-convex objective regions. We introduce MOCHA (Multi-Objective Chebyshev Annealing), which replaces single-objective selection with Chebyshev scalarization - covering the full Pareto front, including non-convex regions - combined with exponential annealing that transitions from exploration to exploitation. In our experiments across six diverse agent skills - where all methods share the same multi-objective mutation operator and baselines receive identical per-objective textual feedback - existing optimizers fail to improve the seed skill on 4 of 6 tasks: 1000 rollouts yield zero progress. MOCHA breaks through on every task, achieving 7.5% relative improvement in mean correctness over the strongest baseline (up to 14.9% on FEVER and 10.4% on TheoremQA) while discovering twice as many more Pareto-optimal skill variants.

</details>


### [205] [Generative Recursive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19376)
*Junyeob Baek,Mingyu Jo,Minsu Kim,Mengye Ren,Yoshua Bengio,Sungjin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出生成式递归推理模型（GRAM），将递归潜在推理转化为概率多轨迹计算，通过随机潜在轨迹实现多假设、多策略和推理时扩展，在结构推理和多解约束满足任务上优于确定性基线模型，并具备无条件生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有递归推理模型（RRMs）主要为确定性的，遵循单一潜在轨迹并收敛到单一预测，缺乏处理多假设、多解路径的能力，限制了其在需要多样化推理场景下的表现。

Method: GRAM将递归潜在推理建模为随机潜在轨迹，支持通过递归深度和平行轨迹采样实现推理时扩展；构建潜变量生成模型，支持条件推理 p_θ(y|x) 和无条件生成 p_θ(x)；使用摊销变分推断进行训练。

Result: 在结构推理和多解约束满足任务上，GRAM优于确定性递归和循环基线模型，并展现出无条件生成能力。

Conclusion: GRAM为未来神经推理系统实现扩展计算提供了一种有前景的概率框架，通过将确定性递归推理扩展为概率多轨迹计算，提升了推理的多样性和灵活性。

Abstract: How should future neural reasoning systems implement extended computation? Recursive Reasoning Models (RRMs) offer a promising alternative to autoregressive sequence extension by performing iterative latent-state refinement with shared transition functions. Yet existing RRMs are largely deterministic, following a single latent trajectory and converging to a single prediction. We introduce \emph{Generative Recursive reAsoning Models (GRAM)}, a framework that turns recursive latent reasoning into probabilistic multi-trajectory computation. GRAM models reasoning as a stochastic latent trajectory, enabling multiple hypotheses, alternative solution strategies, and inference-time scaling through both recursive depth and parallel trajectory sampling. This yields a latent-variable generative model supporting conditional reasoning via $p_θ(y \mid x)$ and, with fixed or absent inputs, unconditional generation via $p_θ(x)$. Trained with amortized variational inference, GRAM improves over deterministic recurrent and recursive baselines on structured reasoning and multi-solution constraint satisfaction tasks, while demonstrating an unconditional generation capability. \href{https://ahn-ml.github.io/gram-website/}{https://ahn-ml.github.io/gram-website}

</details>


### [206] [What and When to Distill: Selective Hindsight Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19447)
*Xiaozhe Li,Tianyi Lyu,Yang Li,Yichuan Ma,Peiji Li,Linyang Li,Qipeng Guo,Dahua Lin,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SERL框架，通过选择性环境重加权学习，利用环境反馈（错误信息、页面变化、观察、参考轨迹等）优化LLM智能体的多轮决策信用分配，在ALFWorld和WebShop上分别达到90.0%和80.1%的成功率，优于现有强化学习和蒸馏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在长程稀疏奖励下难以进行有效信用分配，且多轮交互场景中智能体可获取的丰富环境反馈（如错误信息、页面变化等）未被充分利用。

Method: 提出SERL框架：任务奖励决定更新方向，环境反馈调整动作权重的位置和幅度，聚焦关键动作。系统研究了5种反馈来源和2种插入粒度。

Result: 在ALFWorld上达到90.0%成功率，在WebShop上达到80.1%成功率，优于强RL和蒸馏基线。有依据的、与动作相关的反馈优于不加区分地使用更长或更丰富的上下文。

Conclusion: 环境反馈在关键节点上的选择性使用比简单地增加上下文长度或丰富度更有效，为多轮LLM智能体的信用分配提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning can train LLM agents from sparse task rewards, but long-horizon credit assignment remains challenging: a single success-or-failure signal must be distributed across many actions. Existing methods rely on trajectory-level rewards or proxy signals, without fully leveraging per-step environmental feedback. Multi-turn agent settings are underexplored, where feedback can include error messages, page changes, observations, or reference trajectories. We systematically study five feedback sources and two insertion granularities and introduce SERL, a selective environment-reweighted learning framework. SERL uses the task reward to determine update direction, while environment feedback adjusts placement and magnitude, focusing on critical actions. On ALFWorld and WebShop, SERL achieves 90.0% and 80.1% success, outperforming strong RL and distillation baselines. Analysis shows that grounded, action-relevant feedback at meaningful points consistently outperforms indiscriminate use of longer or richer context.

</details>


### [207] [Beyond Mode Collapse: Distribution Matching for Diverse Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19461)
*Xiaozhe Li,Yang Li,Xinyu Fang,Shengyuan Ding,Peiji Li,Yongkang Chen,Yichuan Ma,Tianyi Lyu,Linyang Li,Dahua Lin,Qipeng Guo,Qingwen Liu,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DMPO方法，通过前向KL散度最小化的近似来防止on-policy强化学习中的模式坍缩问题，在组合优化、数学推理等任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy RL方法（如GRPO）存在模式坍缩问题：一旦发现高奖励解，就会将概率质量集中到该解上，停止探索其他策略。作者证明这是由反向KL最小化的mode-seeking行为导致的。

Method: 提出DMPO（Distribution-Matching Policy Optimization），通过构建基于奖励比例的组级目标分布，然后使策略分布对齐该目标分布，从而实现前向KL最小化的近似，达到mode-covering的效果。

Result: 在NP-Bench文本任务上达到43.9% Quality Ratio（GRPO为40.1%），视觉任务上43.1%（GRPO为38.4%），分别提升9%和12%；数学推理提升2.0%，领域外任务提升2.3%。

Conclusion: 分布匹配是一种实用且 principled 的防止on-policy RL模式坍缩的方法，通过保持多样化训练能够增强跨模态的通用推理能力。

Abstract: On-policy reinforcement learning methods like GRPO suffer from mode collapse: they exhibit reduced solution diversity, concentrating probability mass on a single solution once discovered and ceasing exploration of alternative strategies. We show this stems from reverse KL minimization's mode-seeking behavior, which reinforces the first high-reward trajectory found rather than maintaining a distribution over multiple diverse solutions. We propose DMPO (Distribution-Matching Policy Optimization), which prevents mode collapse through principled approximation of forward KL minimization. DMPO constructs a group level target distribution over sampled trajectories proportional to their rewards, then aligns the policy distribution to this target. This provides mode-covering behavior without requiring sampling from the intractable global target distribution, enabling sustained exploration throughout training. We validate DMPO on NP-hard combinatorial optimization, where exponentially many feasible solutions exist but only a few approach optimality, an ideal testbed for evaluating exploration. DMPO achieves 43.9% Quality Ratio on text-based NP-Bench (vs. GRPO's 40.1%) and 43.1% on vision-based NP-Bench (vs. 38.4%), demonstrating 9% and 12% relative improvements respectively. These gains generalize to mathematical reasoning (+2.0%) and out-of-domain tasks (+2.3%), showing that diversity-preserving training enhances general reasoning capabilities across modalities. Our work establishes distribution matching as a practical, principled approach to preventing mode collapse in on-policy RL, with consistent quality improvements demonstrating sustained exploration across diverse reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [208] [Position: The Turing-Completeness of Real-World Autoregressive Transformers Relies Heavily on Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19514)
*Guanyu Cui,Zhewei Wei,Kun He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文区分了Transformer图灵完备性的两种证明设定：固定系统设定（单一模型加固定上下文管理）和缩放族设定（模型族随输入长度扩展）。现有证明多基于后者，但前者更符合实际LLM部署和图灵完备的标准定义。本文指出缩放族设定仅提供资源界而非真正图灵完备性，并强调上下文管理方法对计算能力的关键影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献常混淆Transformer图灵完备性的两种不同设定，导致对证明结果的误解。本文旨在澄清这一混淆，明确实际LLM部署对应的固定系统设定下Transformer并非传统意义上的图灵完备。

Method: 形式化定义了固定系统设定，对比了缩放族设定与固定系统设定在图灵完备性上的差异，分析了不同上下文管理方法对计算能力的影响。

Result: 缩放族设定下的图灵完备性证明仅提供资源界，不构成真正的图灵完备性；不同上下文管理方法可产生截然不同的计算能力。

Conclusion: 上下文管理是决定实际自回归Transformer计算能力的关键组件，应予以更多关注。

Abstract: Many works make the eye-catching claim that Transformers are Turing-complete. However, the literature often conflates two distinct settings: (i) a fixed Transformer system setting, in which a fixed autoregressive Transformer is coupled with a fixed context-management method to process inputs of different lengths step by step, and (ii) a scaling-family setting, in which a family of different models (with increasing context-window length or numerical precision) is used to handle different input lengths. Existing proofs of Transformer Turing-completeness are frequently established in setting (ii), whereas real-world LLM deployment and the standard notion of Turing-completeness correspond more naturally to setting (i). In this paper, we first formalize the fixed-system setting, thereby providing a concrete characterization of how real-world LLMs operate. We then argue that results proved in the scaling-family setting provide theoretically meaningful resource bounds but do not establish Turing-completeness, thereby clarifying a common misinterpretation of existing results. Finally, we show that different context-management methods can yield sharply different computational power, and we advocate the position that context management is a central component that critically determines the computational power of real-world autoregressive Transformers.

</details>


### [209] [BLINKG: A Benchmark for LLM-Integrated Knowledge Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19518)
*Carla Castedo,Enrique Iglesias,Manuel Lama,Alberto Bugarin-Diz,Maria-Esther Vidal,David Chaves-Fraga*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BLINKG基准，用于评估大语言模型在异构数据源到知识图谱构建中的映射能力。实验表明LLMs在简单场景表现良好，但在复杂场景中仍有限制，并定义了半自动化KG构建的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的知识图谱构建仍依赖大量人工进行语义对齐，LLMs虽有潜力，但缺乏标准化的评估框架来衡量其将数据模式与本体概念建立对应关系的能力。

Method: 提出了BLINKG基准，包含一系列基于真实用例且复杂度递增的评估场景，并对多个最先进的LLMs进行实验评估。

Result: LLMs在知识图谱构建任务中已展现出有前景的能力，但在复杂场景中性能仍然受限。

Conclusion: BLINKG基准能够有效评估当前LLMs在KG构建中的能力，同时定义了实现半自动化LLM驱动的KG构建所需的条件，为该领域开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Generating Knowledge Graphs (KGs) remains one of the most time-consuming and labor-intensive tasks for knowledge engineers, as they need to identify semantic equivalences between input data sources and ontology terms. While declarative solutions (e.g., RML, SPARQL-Anything) have helped to generalize this process, aligning input schema elements with ontology terms still involves intricate transformations and requires considerable manual effort. With the advent of Large Language Models (LLMs), there is growing interest in leveraging their capabilities to assist KG engineers. Although some studies have explored using LLMs to automate KG construction, there is still no standardized framework for assessing how effectively they establish correspondences between data schemes and ontology concepts. Therefore, in this paper, we propose BLINKG, a benchmark designed to evaluate the mapping capabilities of LLMs in constructing KGs from heterogeneous data sources. The benchmark includes a set of scenarios with increasing complexity, based on real-world use cases. We conduct an extensive experimental evaluation of several stateof-the-art LLMs using BLINK and observe that they already offer promising solutions. However, their performance remains limited in complex scenarios. Thanks to this benchmark, we can already assess the current capabilities of LLMs for KG construction. Additionally, we define a set of requirements for achieving (semi)automated (LLM-driven) KG construction, opening new research lines in this area.

</details>


### [210] [Efficient Elicitation of Collective Disagreements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19521)
*Mohamed Ouaguenouni,Felipe Garrido-Lucero,Umberto Grandi,César Hidalgo,Magdalena Tydrichova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"分层矩阵"（plurality matrix）框架，用于识别计算各种分歧度量所需的最少偏好信息。研究发现许多现有分歧度量（如排名方差和分裂性）需要至少3个备选方案的信息（即level 3），证明成对比较不足以捕捉结构性分歧。文章还设计了两种调查协议来估计分层矩阵，平衡参与者数量与认知负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有调查中成对比较无法区分结构性分歧与噪声，而完整排名又对参与者负担过重。作者希望找到一种折中方案，确定计算分歧度量所需的最少偏好信息。

Method: 提出分层矩阵（plurality matrix）概念，记录每个子集S中每个备选方案排第一的概率。定义分歧度量的"层级"（level）为表达该度量所需的最小子集大小。设计两种偏好获取协议来估计分层矩阵。

Result: 证明许多现有分歧度量（如排名方差、分裂性）位于level 3，即成对比较（level 2）不足以表达它们。展示了超越level 3的理论和实验价值。设计了两种偏好获取协议，在参与者数量与认知负荷之间取得平衡。

Conclusion: 成对比较不足以捕捉选民间结构性分歧，需要至少3个备选方案的信息。分层矩阵框架为设计更有效的调查提供了理论依据和实用工具。

Abstract: We analyze the structure of the disagreement among a population of voters over a set of alternatives. Surveys typically ask either for pairwise comparisons, simple and intuitive for participants, or full rankings over alternatives, eliciting the entire voters' preferences. Building on the observation that pairwise comparisons cannot distinguish structural disagreement from noise, we propose a stratified framework to identify the minimal aggregated preference information needed to compute a number of disagreement measures from the literature. Specifically, we introduce the plurality matrix, a generalization of pairwise comparisons that records, for every subset $S$ of alternatives, the probability that each $a \in S$ ranks first in $S$. We define the level of a disagreement measure as the smallest subset size needed to express it, showing that many existing notions, including rank-variance and divisiveness, sit at level $3$, proving that pairwise comparisons are not enough. In addition, we demonstrate the interest of going beyond level $3$ both theoretically and experimentally. To make these results actionable, we design two elicitation protocols to estimate the plurality matrix, exploring the trade-off between the number of required participants and the cognitive load requested to each of them.

</details>


### [211] [Generative-Evaluative Agreement: A Necessary Validity Criterion for LLM-Enabled Adaptive Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19529)
*Grandee Lee,Yue Wang,Che Yee Lye,Luke Peh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出生成-评估一致性（GEA）作为衡量LLM自生成试题、模拟学生作答并评分这一自指闭环有效性的新标准，发现在两阶段自适应评估中模型仅能恢复约一半的预期方差（r=0.698），且存在系统性正向偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当同一个LLM同时承担生成试题、模拟学生作答和评分三项任务时，验证循环存在自我指涉问题，需要建立有效的效度标准来评估这种自指评估的可信度。

Method: 提出生成-评估一致性（GEA）指标，直接测量LLM的评分函数能否恢复其生成函数被指示产生的技能水平，并在两阶段自适应评估场景中进行实证测量。

Result: 模型恢复约一半的预期方差（r=0.698），存在系统性正向偏差；语法可验证技能的GEA较强（r>0.7），而设计层次技能的GEA接近零；低技能高估导致路由阈值附近分数膨胀。

Conclusion: 精细化的、按技能分解的评分标准是增强GEA的主要机制，并提出了补充性的缓解措施。

Abstract: When the same LLM generates assessment items, simulates student responses, and scores them, the validation loop is self-referential. We introduce Generative-Evaluative Agreement (GEA), a validity criterion measuring whether an LLM's scoring function recovers the skill levels its generative function was instructed to produce. In the first direct measurement of GEA on a two-stage adaptive assessment, the model recovers roughly half the intended variance r = 0.698 with systematic positive bias. GEA is strong r > 0.7 for syntactically verifiable skills but near zero for design-level skills, and low-skill overestimation inflates scores near the routing threshold. We argue that granular, skill-decomposed rubrics are the principal proposed mechanism for strengthening GEA and outline complementary mitigations.

</details>


### [212] [Library Drift: Diagnosing and Fixing a Silent Failure Mode in Self-Evolving LLM Skill Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19576)
*Xing Zhang,Yanwei Cui,Guanghui Wang,Ziyuan Li,Wei Qiu,Bing Zhu,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了自进化技能库中的"库漂移"问题——无结果驱动的生命周期管理导致技能无限制积累，引发检索退化、假阳性注入和性能停滞。作者提供了可复现的触发机制、追踪级诊断工具以及最小治理方案（结果驱动淘汰+有界活跃容量+元技能创作先验），将MBPP+ hard-100上的pass@1从0.258提升至0.584（滚动增益+0.328），并通过8项消融实验拆解了各治理机制的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化技能库存在"库漂移"这一静默失败模式：技能无限制积累但缺乏结果驱动的生命周期管理，导致检索退化、假阳性注入和性能停滞。虽然已有实证（SkillsBench上LLM创作技能增益为+0.0pp，人工策展技能增益为+16.2pp），但底层机制尚未被隔离分析。

Method: (1) 可复现触发机制：通过消融实验隔离漂移——禁用技能注入（平坦基底，+0.002）和强制过早淘汰（主动伤害，-0.019）；(2) 追踪级诊断：构建仅追加的证据日志，包含每技能贡献评分、归因判定和路由器参与度指标，在端任务分数恶化前暴露失效；(3) 可验证修复：设计最小治理方案（结果驱动淘汰+有界活跃容量+元技能创作先验），并通过8项消融实验拆解各机制的负载承担情况。

Result: 在MBPP+ hard-100上经过100轮测试，治理方案将held-out pass@1从0.258的基准提升至后期窗口均值0.584（滚动增益+0.328）。8项消融实验明确了哪些治理机制是承重的、哪些是被包含的。

Conclusion: 本文为诊断任何自进化智能体中的库漂移提供了具体操作指南，并验证了最小治理方案的有效性。

Abstract: Self-evolving skill libraries face a silent failure mode we term \emph{library drift}: unbounded skill accumulation without outcome-driven lifecycle management causes retrieval degradation, false-positive injections, and performance stagnation. Recent evaluation confirms the symptom--LLM-authored skills deliver +0.0pp gain while human-curated ones deliver +16.2pp (SkillsBench)--yet the underlying mechanism has not been isolated. We provide (1) a reproducible trigger: ablations that isolate drift--one disables skill injection (flat floor, +0.002), one imposes premature retirement (active harm, $-$0.019); (2) trace-level diagnostics: an append-only evidence log with per-skill contribution scores, attribution verdicts, and router engagement metrics that make the failure visible before it reaches end-task scores; and (3) a verified fix: a minimal governance recipe (outcome-driven retirement + bounded active-cap + meta-skill authoring prior) that lifts held-out pass@1 from a 0.258 baseline to a late-window mean of 0.584 (rolling gain $+$0.328) on MBPP+ hard-100 over 100 rounds. Eight ablations decompose which governance mechanisms are load-bearing and which are subsumed, providing a concrete playbook for diagnosing library drift in any self-evolving agent.

</details>


### [213] [SceneCode: Executable World Programs for Editable Indoor Scenes with Articulated Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19587)
*Puyi Wang,Yuhao Wang,Linjie Li,Zhengyuan Yang,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Yangguang Li,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SceneCode 将自然语言提示编译为可执行的程序化室内世界，通过代码生成物理可交互的仿真资产，实现可追踪、可局部编辑的场景构建流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内场景合成方法将生成内容表示为静态网格，仅从精选资产库继承关节信息，限制了物体级可控性且无法按需生成新的可交互资产。

Method: 提出 SceneCode 框架，包含：1) 房间级智能体将提示转为结构化房屋布局，通过规划器-设计器-评论器循环生成资产请求；2) 五种代码生成策略将请求转为 Blender Python 程序，经执行引导的修复精炼循环验证；3) 持续场景状态注册表链接请求、程序、几何与仿真资产。

Result: 在场景级合成、物体级资产质量、人工评估和下游机器人交互任务上验证，可执行世界程序改善了提示忠实度，生成资产具有更干净的网格结构和可加载的关节元数据。

Conclusion: SceneCode 通过程序化世界生成范式，将场景装配转变为可追踪、可局部编辑的世界构建过程，弥合了静态生成与物理交互之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Indoor scene synthesis underpins embodied AI, robotic manipulation, and simulation-based policy evaluation, where a useful scene must specify not only what the environment looks like, but also how its objects are structured. Existing pipelines, however, typically represent generated content as static meshes and inherit articulation only from curated asset libraries, which limits object-level controllability and prevents new interactable assets from being produced on demand. We address this gap by formulating physically interactable indoor scene synthesis as programmatic world generation, and present SceneCode, a framework that compiles a natural language prompt into an executable, code-driven indoor world rather than a collection of opaque meshes. A room-level agentic backbone first turns the prompt into a structured house layout and emits per-object AssetRequests through a planner--designer--critic loop. Each request is then routed to one of five code-generation strategies and converted into a synthesized part-wise Blender Python programs that are validated through an execution-guided repair-and-refine loop. The resulting programs are compiled into simulation-ready assets, and exported as SDF for physics simulation. A persistent scene-state registry links object requests, executable programs, rendered geometry, and simulation assets, turning scene assembly into a traceable and locally editable world-building process. We evaluate SceneCode across scene-level synthesis, object-level asset quality, human judgment, and downstream robot interaction. Results show that executable world programs improve prompt-faithful indoor scene generation and produce assets with cleaner mesh structure, and simulator-loadable articulation metadata. Project page: https://scene-code.github.io/.

</details>


### [214] [Towards Multi-Model LLM Schedulers: Empirical Insights into Offloading and Preemption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19593)
*Mert Yildiz,Pietro Spadaccino,Alexey Rolich,Francesca Cuomo,Andrea Baiocchi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在多模型、异构硬件环境下LLM服务面临的资源调度挑战，重点分析了层卸载和抢占对不同模型的性能影响，发现卸载导致非线性的解码吞吐下降（小模型更敏感），抢占开销主要由模型状态重载而非KV缓存传输主导，并受序列长度和互联带宽影响，最终提出了未来调度器应具备的关键特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统主要针对单一模型优化吞吐量，而在多模型、异构硬件的实际部署场景下，资源分配、调度和抢占策略的研究相对不足，尤其是在GPU内存受限需要CPU-GPU混合卸载的情况下。

Method: 通过实证研究，系统性地测试不同LLM在不同硬件平台上的行为，重点测量层卸载和抢占对解码吞吐量的影响，分析模型大小、序列长度、互联带宽等因素的作用。

Result: 卸载导致强非线性和模型依赖的解码吞吐下降，小模型对GPU驻留减少更敏感；抢占开销主要由模型状态重载主导而非KV缓存传输，且不同模型和硬件平台间差异显著；序列长度和互联带宽放大数据传输和执行效率问题。

Conclusion: 未来调度器必须考虑模型特定的卸载敏感性、工作负载特征以及抢占和数据传输的成本结构，以支持异构多模型工作负载下的高效LLM服务。

Abstract: Modern deployments of Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly require serving multiple models with diverse architectures, sizes, and specialization on shared, heterogeneous hardware. This setting introduces new challenges for resource allocation, dispatching, and scheduling, particularly under GPU memory constraints where partial CPU-GPU offloading and preemption become necessary. While existing systems primarily optimize throughput for a single model, comparatively little work addresses multi-model scheduling under these conditions. In this paper, we present an empirical study of how different LLMs behave across hardware platforms, focusing on the performance implications of layer offloading and preemption. We show that offloading leads to strongly non-linear and model-dependent degradation in decode throughput, with smaller models exhibiting sharper sensitivity to reduced GPU residency. We further demonstrate that preemption incurs substantial overhead, largely dominated by model state reload rather than key-value cache transfer, and that this cost varies significantly across models and hardware platforms. Additionally, we highlight the role of sequence length and interconnect bandwidth in amplifying data movement and execution inefficiencies. Based on these findings, we identify a set of key features that future schedulers must consider, including model-specific offloading sensitivity, workload characteristics, and the cost structure of preemption and data transfer. These insights provide guidance for the design of next-generation LLM serving systems capable of efficiently managing heterogeneous, multi-model workloads with hybrid CPU-GPU execution.

</details>


### [215] [EMO-BOOST: Emotion-Augmented Audio-Visual Features for Improved Generalization in Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19630)
*Aritra Marik,Marcel Klemt,Anna Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Emo-Boost框架，通过融合基于情感的高层语义线索与底层检测方法，提升深度伪造检测对未知篡改类型的泛化能力，在FakeAVCeleb数据集上AUC提升2.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI技术的快速发展使得新型深度伪造技术不断涌现，传统方法难以收集所有篡改类型的数据进行训练，因此需要提升检测模型对未见过的深度伪造类型的泛化能力。

Method: 提出Emo-Boost多模态深度伪造检测框架，融合现成的RGB和音频检测器与基于情感的检测器EmoForensics。EmoForensics利用视觉和音频情感识别模块，建模音视频流中情感表征的帧内和跨模态时序一致性。

Result: EmoForensics与底层方法捕获互补信号，将两者结合可提升跨篡改泛化AUC 2.1%（在FakeAVCeleb数据集上）。

Conclusion: 高层语义线索（如情感）能够支持底层方法提升对未知类型深度伪造的泛化检测能力，多模态融合策略有效。

Abstract: With every advancement in generative AI models, forensics is under increasing pressure. The constant emergence of new generation techniques makes it impossible to collect data for each manipulation to train a deepfake detection model. Thus, generalizing to deepfakes unseen during training is one of the major challenges in current deepfake detection research. To tackle this challenge, we employ high-level semantic cues and argue that these cues can support low-level focused approaches in generalizing to unseen types of manipulations. In this work, we study emotions as a high-level semantic cue. We propose Emo-Boost, a multimodal deepfake detection framework that fuses an off-the-shelf RGB- and acoustic-focused deepfake detector with our emotion-based deepfake detector EmoForensics. EmoForensics utilises vision and audio emotion recognition modules and models intra- and inter-modal temporal consistency in emotion representations from an audio-visual stream. We found that EmoForensics and the low-level focused method capture complementary signals. Consequently, combining both signals in EmoBoost enhances the average cross-manipulation generalization AUC by 2.1% on FakeAVCeleb.

</details>


### [216] [Pseudocode-Guided Structured Reasoning for Automating Reliable Inference in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19663)
*Weicong Ni,Tianbao Jiang,Linlin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PStar框架，通过自适应选择结构化伪代码推理路径，显著降低VLM的幻觉率，在POPE和MMStar上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在机器人自动化决策中易产生幻觉，导致安全风险，且真实世界任务具有开放性和难度多变性，现有方法缺乏鲁棒的自适应推理策略。
